> **🛑 WALK RULE — verify the artifact that RENDERS the surface under test.**
> Measured 2026-08-11: **33 of 40** red rows established "the running artifact"
> from `catalyst-api`'s `/api/v1/version`, and only 6 mentioned `catalyst-ui` at
> all. For every console-rendered clause — the whole topology block, W1, W2 —
> that is the **wrong binary**. `catalyst-ui` rolls independently: it served a
> 2026-08-02 build (`fad88bd`) for eight days behind a rollout deadlock while
> `catalyst-api` rolled six times in one night. Those stamps never checked
> whether the fix under test was in the bundle being rendered.
>
> * A **console/UI** clause is verified against the **served bundle** — fetch the
>   asset and grep it for the fix's marker. Not the API version, not `git log`.
> * An **API/controller** clause is verified against that component's image.
> * **Never cite an image sha as a row's evidence.** The deploy treadmill rolls
>   roughly every 30 minutes; a sha is stale before the next reader arrives.
>   Cite what you observed on the rendered surface.

## Status icons — the canonical definitions

These six are the ENTIRE vocabulary. `scripts/uat-audit.py` fails the build on any
verdict glyph outside this set, because an uncounted glyph is silently dropped by
`uat-backfill.py` and then reads downstream as "never walked" — which turns an
unreadable verdict into a missing pass. Four rows carried a stray `◑` that way, and
`⏳` dropped thirteen more, before the guard existed.

| icon | name | means | what moves it |
|---|---|---|---|
| `✅` | ⏳ | Every clause in the test case was measured on the CURRENT env and held, with a control showing the check could have come out the other way. | Nothing — until the env is replaced or the clause changes, when it returns to ☐. |
| `❌` | FAIL | Measured on the current env and it did NOT hold. A named, reproducible defect. This is the honest home for anything undelivered — a feature that has not shipped is a FAIL, not a block. | A fix, deployed, then re-walked. |
| `⚠️` | PARTIAL | **Working state only — never a terminal verdict.** Some clauses proven, others not. A test case whose clause does not fully hold is a FAILING test case, so a row may not be REPORTED in this state: resolve it to ✅ or ❌ before a convergence cycle is captured. It exists so a walk-in-progress can be saved mid-flight. | Closing the named residual (→ ✅) or accepting it as the failure it is (→ ❌). |
| `⛔` | SUPERSEDED | The test case cannot be answered as written — its subject no longer exists, or it asserts a value the schema forbids. **Not** a parking space for hard work. | Retire-and-REPLACE, one-for-one, recorded in `uat-retirements.csv`. |
| `☐` | NOTRUN | Never walked on this env. Includes a freshly replaced clause, which must NOT inherit the retired row's verdict. | Walking it. |
| `⏳` | PENDING | **Working state only — never a terminal verdict**, same rule as PARTIAL. Written by `scripts/reset-uat.py` when a new env flushes prior evidence, to mark awaiting-re-walk as distinct from never-walked. A row may not be REPORTED in this state: it is outside the PASS/FAIL vocabulary a reader counts, so a row left here is invisible rather than merely undecided — 3 rows were uncounted that way. | Re-walking on the live env, then ✅ or ❌. |

**The denominator is STONE at 286.** Retiring a row always carries a replacement so
the count never moves; `scripts/uat-snapshot.py` refuses any other total. A
floating denominator makes every percentage incomparable across cycles, and it has
drifted before.

**Evidence is per-env.** A new Sovereign flushes ALL prior evidence, so the score
resets to near-zero on every fresh prov (measured: 133 green 2026-07-30 → 25 on
2026-08-03). A drop across an env boundary is the ledger working; a drop WITHIN one
env is a regression. `scripts/uat-drift-guard.py` refuses a ✅ row that cites a
wiped env.

**Env:** live omantel.biz Sovereign, dep `91dc05917e44d1c1`, 2-region converged (region-a 62/62 HRs · region-b 56/56 HRs, me-east-215-a + me-east-215-b). All verdicts below are against THIS env unless a row carries its own labeled prior-env evidence.

**The single decisive gate:** kom4dc EIP quota is **10** (free 3); a fresh post-fix 2-region prov needs **6** EIPs — a HARD WALL with zero orphans to reclaim. The Huawei ELB round-robins :443 across both regions' hostNetwork cilium-envoy, which is why every funnel hostname sits near 50 percent. SEVERITY, measured rather than inferred: a real browser fares far worse than the curl ratio suggests, because once Chromium's per-origin HTTP/1.1 socket lands on region B it STAYS there - this walk recorded 8 consecutive 503 page loads on marketplace.hw293.omantel.biz while fresh-TCP curl against the very same path returned 6/12 x 200 in parallel. Evidence docs/sessions/2026-08-10/evidence/hw293-funnel-region-split-503.txt ‖ hw292-2026-08-06T01:50Z ‖ COLLAPSE-ONTO-ONE-PATH RE-CONFIRMED on a live customer Org: https://console.uatco.omani.homes/ answers 200 and serves the SAME single catalyst-ui door as the Sovereign console — the PIN form 'Sign in / Enter your email to receive a 6-digit PIN' scoped to 'console.uatco.omani.homes', and signing in there (PIN issued + verified) lands on the per-Org console at /apps. CAUSE IS A PER-REGION SPLIT, not a wildcard collision: region-A kube-system/cilium-gateway-console carries listeners console-https-uatco/console-http-uatco for *.uatco.omani.homes, region-B's does NOT (its 8 listeners carry *.r17probe.omani.homes instead) and region-B holds ZERO uatco HTTPRoutes. ORPHAN NOTE: the r17probe listener is dead weight — GET /api/v1/organizations shows r17probe state='deleted' with all 6 steps 'pending', yet its region-B gateway listener survives the Org's deletion ‖hw290-2026-07-26: hw290 2026-07-26T16:14-16:17Z ‖ COLLAPSE-ONTO-ONE-PATH: CONFIRMED. An anonymous browser at https://console.<slug> is served the single catalyst-ui door which immediately runs the per-Org silent OIDC check — console.acme-corp.omani.homes -> HTTP 200, final URL https://auth.acme-corp.omani.homes/realms/sovereign/protocol/openid-connect/auth?response_type=code&client_id=catalyst-ui&redirect_uri=https%3A%2F%2Fconsole.acme-corp.omani.homes%2Fauth%2Fcallback&scope=openid+profile+email&state=…&code_challenge_method=S256&prompt=none; console.walk-two.omani.rest and console.delta-corp.omani.homes reproduce identically on their own auth hosts. Shots .../scratchpad/fw-R16-console.acme-corp.omani.homes.png, fw-R16-console.walk-two.omani.rest.png, fw-R16-console.delta-corp.omani.homes.png. WHY PARTIAL, two live gaps: (a) the door only answers ~50% of the time — pinned to 212.72.24.82, console.acme-corp 5/10 200, console.delta-corp 4/10, console.walk-two 4/10, rest ERR_CONNECTION_RESET, while console.hw290.omani.works on the SAME IP is 10/10 200 and raw TCP 212.72.24.82:443 is 10/10 OK (delta-corp needed an in-run retry before it loaded); SNI-selective failure of the *.<orgslug> wildcard listeners on Gateway kube-system/cilium-gateway-console. (b) console.gamma-corp.omani.homes does not resolve at all (NXDOMAIN) and has no gateway listener — that Organization has no console door whatsoever (it is also the CR-less Org: organizations.orgs.openova.io/gamma-corp NotFound). ‖ —  ‖ 2026-08-07 SOURCE ADJUDICATION of gap (a), and it REFUTES the hypothesis this row's own evidence invites. The obvious read of 'region-A carries console-https-uatco, region-B does not' is 'the org-controller runs single-region and never writes region-B'. That is WRONG, and this row's own text contains the counter-evidence: region-B's cilium-gateway-console carries 8 listeners INCLUDING per-Org ones for *.r17probe.omani.homes. A controller that could not reach region-B could not have written those either. So region-B IS reachable and HAS been written; what it holds is a per-Org listener set from an EARLIER Org that was never updated for uatco and never cleaned up for r17probe. Anyone fixing this by 'making the controller multi-region' would be fixing a mechanism that is already there. WHAT THE SOURCE SHOWS. tenant_console_tls.go appends the console-https-<slug>/console-http-<slug> pair idempotently to the SHARED kube-system/cilium-gateway-console; teardown IS wired - teardownTenantNetworking (tenant_networking_teardown.go:112) is called from organization_controller.go:455 and is held open by the dedicated TenantNetworkingFinalizer precisely so the cascade cannot be skipped when no other finalizer is present (#4459). So neither the write nor the delete path is missing code. The r17probe listener surviving an Org whose API record reads state='deleted' is therefore NOT a teardown-logic bug - it is #5426 (Organization delete never runs the finalizer cascade: no REST route, no UI action, so the CR is never deleted and the finalizer never fires). The listener outlives the Org because nothing ever asked it to die. CLASSIFICATION: gap (a) belongs to the #5635 family (single-region resource behind a two-region shared EIP, ~50% fresh-TCP failure) - same mechanism, applied to the console listener instead of the app namespace - and the orphan half is #5426. Not filed as a new issue; a third ticket for a mechanism twice-filed would inflate the backlog without adding a fix. NOTE THE MEASUREMENT DISCIPLINE THIS ROW ALREADY GOT RIGHT and that made the adjudication possible: fresh-TCP curl with --no-keepalive. Under HTTP/2 the browser pins one backend and the same host reads 10/10, which is how a 50% split hides. ‖ hw292-2026-08-10 PLATFORM REGRESSION, DATED. Same dated cause as row 87 (#5246). The door-collapse claim itself still holds — one catalyst-ui door, BSS a path on it; what fails is 'lands 200', at 5/10. ‖ NO-PARTIALS DISPOSITION 2026-08-10 — RECLASSIFIED ⚠️ -> ❌. Every clause in a test case must hold for the row to pass; this row's evidence NAMES a clause that does not. A partial is a FAILING test case with some passing parts, and carrying it as its own class let real failures sit in a comfort bucket where no backlog counted them — 27 rows were doing exactly that. NOTHING WAS RE-MEASURED HERE and no verdict got worse: the residual named above is the same residual, in the same words, with the same evidence. Only the class changed, from one that reads as 'nearly there' to one that reads as 'does not pass'. This moves ZERO rows out of green, so it is neither progress nor regression — it is the removal of a hiding place. The row closes when the named residual closes. ‖ PARTITION 2026-08-10: ENV-STATE — needs an environment SHAPE this Sovereign does not have (pre-cutover window, single region, or a destructive prov->wipe cycle). Not a code gap and not re-walkable here. ‖ RE-TAGGED 2026-08-10: NEEDS-CODE -> DEPLOY-GATED. The fix for this row is now MERGED on main as 0439fbea8 (PR #5957). It is not running on hw292 — the deployed images pre-date it — so the verdict correctly stays ❌ and a re-walk here would reproduce it. What changed is the CLASS: this row no longer waits on engineering, it waits on a roll or a fresh prov. The merge SHA is the on-disk artifact. ‖ CITATION CORRECTED 2026-08-10: the SHA in the re-tag above read 83f2a5521, which is the docs-only LEDGER commit itself (#5962 — docs/ledger/UAT.md + WBS-TO-100.md + uat-raw.csv and nothing else); the same SHA was pasted onto ELEVEN rows under three different PR numbers, so anyone who checked the warrant would have found a prose diff and concluded the whole re-tag was invented. Verdict and partition are unchanged — only the pointer was wrong. Now enforced pre-merge by scripts/check-uat-fix-sha-citations.py, which fails a citation whose commit is docs-only or whose subject carries a different PR number. ‖ PARTITION CONTRADICTION RESOLVED 2026-08-10 — this cell carried TWO partition labels at once: "PARTITION: ENV-STATE" and, after it, "RE-TAGGED: NEEDS-CODE -> DEPLOY-GATED", which also names a class the row was never in. DEPLOY-GATED is the correct one and ENV-STATE is refuted on the row's own evidence: the residual is not an environment SHAPE this Sovereign lacks, it is that the per-Org console listener pair is written to region A only while the console EIP's backend pool spans both regions, so roughly half of fresh TCP connections reach a region with no listener for that SNI and are reset. That is a code defect with a merged fix — 0439fbea8 (PR #5957) makes every write in tenant_console_tls.go fan over consoleRegionTargets, built from the cutover-secondary-kubeconfigs the #5359 bridge already materializes, and carries the teardown and the #5395 postcondition verifier over the same set. hw292 needs no different shape; it needs that image. ‖ RE-TAGGED 2026-08-11: DEPLOY-GATED -> NEEDS-CODE — the cited fix 0439fbea8 (PR #5957) IS in the artifact this env runs. The organization-controller image is 4d1da63 and 0439fbea8 is an ancestor of it, so the row's own warrant — that the deployed images pre-date the fix — is false here. Measured read-only against BOTH region kubeconfigs on 2026-08-11: region A kube-system/cilium-gateway-console carries console-https-hw293walkone and console-https-hw293walktwo, region B carries ONLY the three Sovereign hostname pairs and ZERO per-Org listeners. That is the exact state #5957 exists to prevent, reproduced on top of #5957. ROOT CAUSE READ AT THE EDIT SITE rather than inferred: tenant_console_tls_regions.go builds its region set from the #5359 bridge Secret catalyst/cutover-secondary-kubeconfigs and cross-checks it against kube-system/cilium-clustermesh as an independent witness; on this env NEITHER object exists — clustermesh is established (clustermesh-apiserver 1/1) but publishes clustermesh-apiserver-*-cert instead, and the cutover chart is still installing per #6004 — so the witness declares zero remote regions and the file's own documented single-region path takes over. Its comment says a Sovereign with no ClusterMesh Secret 'behaves EXACTLY as before this file existed', which is what a 2-region Sovereign is now doing. The fix is deployed and inert, so what is owed is engineering, not a roll. |

WHY THE CHAIN HAS NOT RUN, named rather than assumed: the deployment record latched `status: failed` (Phase 1 concluded `failed` on ONE HelmRelease out of 67 — and that one was `self-sovereign-cutover` itself, at chart 0.1.177, on the #6004 1 MiB release-Secret limit), so handover never fired: `handoverFiredAt` is `null` on the record read from the mothership PVC. `fireHandover` runs only under `OutcomeReady && finalStatus=="ready"` and `MintHandoverToken` 409s outside `{ready, adopted}`; those two are the ONLY producers of the Sovereign-side handover marker `secret/catalyst/tofu-phase0-archive`, so the in-cluster auto-trigger stays at 425 handover-incomplete and the level-triggered cutover reconciler returns silently on `!sealed` every 120s (its start line is the only `[CUTOVER-RECONCILE]` entry in the Sovereign catalyst-api log). The single heal path, `shouldConvergedLateRescue`, pre-filtered on the FROZEN `Phase1Outcome` and excluded `failed`, so the live census that would prove recovery was never run — a latch, tracked as #6082 with the gate extended + guarded. `markPhase1Done` and the handover fire both live in the MOTHER's catalyst-api, which still runs image tag `ghcr.io/openova-io/openova/catalyst-api:b2294fd`. ‖ CORRECTION 2026-08-10 (#6082): the earlier clause "the trigger is operator-gated AND requires handover ... no Sovereign-side action reaches this row" conflated two independent entry points and is WRONG on the second half. Only `source="internal"` (the chart's Helm post-install hook) is handover-gated — `cutover_internal.go:553` reads `if source == "internal" && requireHandoverForCutover()`. The operator CTA `POST /api/v1/sovereign/cutover/start` (`HandleCutoverStart`, cutover.go:2378) performs NO handover-seal check at any point: it reads the status ConfigMap, short-circuits on `cutoverComplete==true`, discovers the 11 step ConfigMaps, claims the run flag and starts the engine with `operatorRetry=true`. The console CTA is rendered whenever `!cutoverComplete && !inFlight` (`SovereigntyCard.tsx`, mounted at the sovereign console's Settings → Sovereignty section) with no handover condition. Every precondition it needs is already satisfied on this env: `bp-self-sovereign-cutover` `Ready=True` at 0.1.179, all 11 `cutover-step-*` ConfigMaps present in namespace `catalyst`, and catalyst-api's cutover namespace resolving to that same default. So this row IS walkable on hw293 today from the Sovereign console — it does NOT require an environment that reached handover. ‖ PARTITION 2026-08-10: ENV-STATE — re-tagged out of NEEDS-CODE. Not NEEDS-CODE (no source change is owed; the chart is present and Ready) and not DEPLOY-GATED (the artifact is running here). THE CORRECTION DOES NOT MOVE THE VERDICT: the chain has not started on this env (all 11 result keys empty, `cutoverComplete=false`, `progressPercent=0`), so the row fails under the 8-step and 11-step wording alike. |

CONTROL sharing the suspect property: uat50-ahs-pg, a genuinely active-hot-standby Application on the same CRD and the same console, holds spec.placement as an OBJECT {mode: active-hot-standby, regions: [...]} and spec.parameters.topology.mode active-hot-standby. That 503 is Harbor's SERVER-SIDE fetch of https://auth.hw293.omantel.biz/.well-known/openid-configuration hitting the region-B envoy, whose `auth` HTTPRoute has **zero endpoints**: keycloak-0 in region B has been Init:0/1 for 5h on `MountVolume.SetUp failed for volume "keycloak-secrets": secret "keycloak-database-secret" not found` (#6016), and half of every fresh connection to the shared gateway EIP lands there (#6040). VACUITY GUARD: the admin group's `CREATE_*` system permissions are the RIGHT to create a connection, not a connection — a permission-only check is a FAIL. | hw293-2026-08-10 | ❌ | RETIRED AND REPLACED 2026-08-10. Ground: the old clause ("Guacamole opens signed in, the connections list renders — no Guacamole login form") is a DUPLICATE of row 35 ("Guacamole bare URL → lands on the Guacamole connections list, signed-in; no Tomcat 404, no /guacamole/ login page"), and row 35 is ✅ on THIS env. Both were measured on 2026-08-08 and they disagree, so the disagreement is a method gap, not a platform gap: row 35 is a LIVE BROWSER WALK (Playwright, dep 1c56518035a83e03) recording HTTP 200 at https://guacamole.hw292.omani.works/guacamole/#/, title 'Apache Guacamole', 0 password inputs, no Tomcat 404, no /guacamole/ login page, and the body rendering 'RECENT CONNECTIONS emrah.baysal@openova.io / No recent connections. / ALL CONNECTIONS' — the connections list, signed in, rendered. Row 115's ❌ came from curl (302 on 4/4) plus chart-version archaeology, neither of which can see a rendered list at all. A weaker method must not overturn a browser walk on the same env on the same day. NOT deleted — the slot is reused so the denominator stays frozen at 286, and the full swap is in docs/ledger/uat-retirements.csv. ‖ WHAT THE OLD CLAUSE WAS REACHING FOR, kept: the finding underneath the ❌ was real and is now the clause — guacamole_connection = 0 rows. That half is NOT covered by row 35, which asserts only the landing. It is a MISSING PRODUCER, established from source 2026-08-10 (edaa615a8): nothing in this repository creates a Guacamole connection. platform/guacamole/chart/files/postgresql/001-create-schema.sql:109 creates the table empty; the only thing the chart seeds is identity + permission — chart/templates/jdbc-schema-seed-job.yaml:155-181 creates the admin USER_GROUP and grants it ADMINISTER plus five CREATE_* system permissions, which is the right to create connections and not a connection. The GuacamoleConnection CRD + relay-controller the README used to describe do not exist (no Go type, no CRD manifest, no reconciler, no chart template). A seeded row also needs a WIRE fix, not just an INSERT: bp-k8s-ws-proxy rejects any upgrade without X-Catalyst-Timestamp + X-Catalyst-HMAC (core/cmd/k8s-ws-proxy/DESIGN.md:32-37, 401 in its failure table) and guacd's kubernetes protocol handler cannot emit them, so a naive INSERT would render a connection that 401s on click — passing this row while the feature stayed broken. Deliberately NOT seeded for that reason. Reset to unwalked because a new clause has never been walked; inheriting the old verdict would be fabrication. ‖ PARTITION 2026-08-10: NEEDS-CODE — the producer does not exist. This row is engineering work (the INSERT path plus the guacd-to-ws-proxy wire), not a walk; a re-walk cannot change it. ‖ hw293-2026-08-10 FIRST WALK OF THE REPLACEMENT CLAUSE, ON A FRESH PROV — PROVEN FAIL, measured against the table the clause names. Read-only psql on the live CNPG instance guacamole/guacamole-pg-1, database guacamole_db: SELECT count(*) FROM guacamole_connection returns 0, and SELECT count(*) FROM guacamole_connection_group returns 0. So the ALL CONNECTIONS list has nothing to render, on an env that is 82 minutes old and has never been touched by a walker. THE ROW'S OWN VACUITY GUARD IS WHAT THE DATABASE SHOWS: guacamole_entity holds exactly two rows — emrah.baysal@openova.io (USER) and sovereign-admins (USER_GROUP) — and guacamole_system_permission grants that group ADMINISTER plus CREATE_CONNECTION, CREATE_CONNECTION_GROUP, CREATE_SHARING_PROFILE, CREATE_USER, CREATE_USER_GROUP. That is the RIGHT to create a connection and not a connection, which the clause declares a FAIL, so the guard fires exactly as written. THE MISSING PRODUCER IS VISIBLE AS A RUNNING OBJECT, not only in source: CronJob guacamole-bp-guacamole-admin-enroll runs every minute and its latest Job completed 4s with the log '[jdbc-seed] db reachable / [jdbc-seed] enrolled 0 user(s) into sovereign-admins'. The only recurring writer the chart ships enrols IDENTITY. Nothing on this Sovereign creates a connection row. NOT A DEPLOY GAP: the platform tier here is healthy — guacamole-server 1/1, guacd 1/1, guacamole-pg-1 1/1, and the guacamole.hw293.omantel.biz HTTPRoute present via oidc-gate — so this is the producer being absent, not a wedged rollout. #5992 removed a fabricated embed URL and did not add an INSERT path; the state it left behind is the state measured here. DELIBERATELY NOT SEEDED BY THIS WALK: a bare INSERT would render a connection that 401s on click, because bp-k8s-ws-proxy requires X-Catalyst-Timestamp + X-Catalyst-HMAC on upgrade and guacd's kubernetes handler cannot emit them — passing the row while the feature stayed broken. The row needs the INSERT path AND that wire, and remains engineering work. ‖ TICKET RE-POINTED 2026-08-11 ([#6111](https://github.com/openova-io/openova/issues/6111)) #3642 -> [#5991](https://github.com/openova-io/openova/issues/5991) — CLAUSE AND RESULT CELL UNTOUCHED. #3642 is CLOSED (2026-06-23) and is 'NS#1: migrate the 7 host-placed apps (grafana/harbor/keycloak/gitea/openbao/newapi/guacamole) from host into the mgmt vCluster' — a different subject entirely, so a reader following the old link landed on a finished vCluster migration and learned nothing about the missing Guacamole connection producer. [#5991](https://github.com/openova-io/openova/issues/5991) is OPEN and is titled for this exact row: 'Guacamole connections list is empty by construction: no producer exists, and the session API returned a fabricated connectionId + unreachable embedURL (UAT row 115)'. VERDICT UNCHANGED: this row is VALID BUT MISDIAGNOSED — the clause is right and the tracker was wrong. |
